## 堆疊標注 · 堆疊限制

**id**　8e24e5aff3cb05cb05-8

**english**　Stack labels

**chapter**　
- 幫助
- 地圖和場景
- 創作地圖和場景
- 文本
- 使用 Maplex 標注引擎放置標注
- 標注自適應策略

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/help/mapping/text/stack-labels.html#F39

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/mapping/text/stack-labels.html

**text**　您可以設置行數和每行字符數的限制，以控制堆疊標注的形狀。 這些限制可用于防止堆疊較短的標注，同時允許堆疊較長的標注。 如果無法滿足給定標注的堆疊字符限制，則將盡可能接近限制地分割該標注。
最大行數指定標注可分割成的最大行數（1 至 50）。 注意： 選擇街道放置樣式后，Maplex 標注引擎會使用街道特殊堆疊規則，該規則不可進行編輯。 當使用彎曲放置樣式時，標注僅堆疊成兩行。
每行的最小字符數指定堆疊標注最短部分必須具有的最小字符數（1 至 8）。 例如，如果此參數設置為 3，則不會堆疊標注 St David，即使空格字符用作堆疊字符也是如此。
每行的最大字符數指定標注的某一給定行可具有的最大字符數（2 至 80）。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/help/mapping/text/stack-labels.html#F39

## 堆疊標注 · 堆疊限制

**id**　8e24e5aff3cb05cb05-9

**english**　Stack labels

**chapter**　
- 幫助
- 地圖和場景
- 創作地圖和場景
- 文本
- 使用 Maplex 標注引擎放置標注
- 標注自適應策略

**kind**　有序步驟

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/latest/help/mapping/text/stack-labels.html#F39

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/mapping/text/stack-labels.html

**steps**　
1. 確保啟用 Maplex 標注引擎。
2. 確保按標注列出是顯示內容窗格的活動方式。 單擊按標注列出選項卡 。
3. 在內容窗格中選擇標注分類，然后單擊標注選項卡。
4. 在標注選項卡的標注放置組中，單擊標注放置屬性 。
5. 在標注窗格中，單擊位置，然后單擊自適應策略選項卡 。
6. 展開堆棧。
7. 選中堆疊標注復選框。 選擇街道放置樣式后，無法編輯堆疊參數。 Maplex 標注引擎使用街道特殊堆疊規則。 當使用彎曲放置樣式時，標注僅堆疊成兩行。
8. 指定用于堆疊長標注的首選項。 選中或取消選中首選堆疊長標注復選框 - 如果選中該復選框，則長標注將在相關區域內緊湊堆疊，這些區域中不存在影響標注放置并需要堆疊才能適應的沖突。 在對采用街道放置樣式的線進行標注時，該選項不可用。
9. 指定要使用的水平對齊：選擇最佳、限制左或右、限制左、限制右或限制中心。 默認情況下，空格字符和逗號已被輸入到分隔符列表中。 空格字符是特殊字符，可通過按空格鍵進行創建。 單詞 space 隨即顯示在此列表中。
10. 要添加其他分隔符，請單擊新建并輸入堆疊字符。 要移除堆疊字符，請選擇字符然后單擊移除。 對于每個堆疊分隔符，完成以下操作： 選中或取消選中可見復選框 - 如果未選中，堆疊字符將不可見并從堆疊標注中移除。 僅當堆疊字符用于堆疊文本字符串時，才會移除此字符。 選中或取消選中強制分割復選框 - 如果選中它，則無論標注在一行上是否有足夠的空間，遇到堆疊字符時都會分割所有標注。 選中或取消選中分割之后復選框 - 如果已選中，則在堆疊字符之后分割標注。 使用移除前導和尾隨分隔符復選框，指定是保留還是移除文本字符串開頭和結尾的堆疊字符。
11. 指定標注分類的堆疊限制。 最大行數指定標注可分割成的最大行數（1 至 50）。 當使用彎曲放置樣式時，標注僅堆疊成兩行。 每行的最小字符數指定堆疊標注最短部分必須具有的最小字符數（1 至 8）。 每行的最大字符數指定標注的某一給定行可具有的最大字符數（2 至 80）。

**content_version**　3.7

**source_language**　zh-cn

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/zh-cn/arcgis-pro/3.7/help/mapping/text/stack-labels.html#F39

## 重疊方法 · 重疊方法

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-1

**english**　Overlay methods

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 分析和資料處理
- 空間分析器
- 建模者

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**text**　重疊方法將多個加權轉換輸入弧形組合成一個單一輸出適合性圖,使用多種多標準決定分析(MCDA)重疊法。 合適性模型必須捕捉主體與標準之間的相互作用。 轉換標準可以確定對象如何在標準范圍內對數值作出反應。 重疊方法允許您捕捉對象在標準之間的相互作用。 類似變換, 每種重疊方法都單獨結合了加權轉換標準, 使你能夠了解該主題在標準之間的特定相互作用。 如果模型能夠更好地捕捉獨特的相互作用, 結果會更準確。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The overlay methods combine the multiple weighted transformed input rasters into a single output suitability map using various Multicriteria Decision Analysis (MCDA) overlay approaches.\nA suitability model must capture the interactions of the subject to the criteria. Transforming the criteria identifies how the subject responds to the values within the criteria. Overlay methods allow you to capture the subject’s interaction between the criteria.\nSimilar to the transformations, each overlay method uniquely combines the weighted transformed criteria allowing you to realize the subject’s particular interactions between the criteria. If the model can better capture the unique interactions, the results will be more accurate.", "section": "Overlay methods"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

## 重疊方法 · 選擇一種方法

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-2

**english**　Overlay methods

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 分析和資料處理
- 空間分析器
- 建模者

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#BGT

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**text**　您可以從以下重疊方法參數選項中選擇:加權和——當標準值高時使用能夠平衡低數值。這是最常見的方法,因為通常你想要通過同時添加加權轉換標準來取得總分。這是默認方法。加權轉換標準高時使用加權轉換標準,不能補償其他標準中的低值。單低值將降低最后適切性,因為這是一種多倍法,即加權轉換標準值作為每個加權轉換標準值的引號應用。最大-僅使用一個加權轉換標準的高值就足以使某個地點適合。最小值是指加權轉換標準中單一低值時,應取消某一地點的資格。上移 - 用于為每項標準分配百分率影響, 加到100 。 結果被重新縮放到定義的適配尺度。 當參數值的重量被設置標簽設定為乘數時, 所分配的加權將按百分比影響內部重新計算 。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "You can choose from the following Overlay method parameter options:\nWeighted sum—Use when high values in a criterion can balance out low values. This is the most common method because often you want an overall score by adding weighted transformed criteria together. This is the default method.\nWeighted geometric mean—Use when high values in a weighted transformed criterion cannot compensate for low values in other criteria. A single low value will reduce the final suitability because this is a multiplicative method, in which the weights are applied as exponents to each weighted transformed criterion value.\nMaximum—Use when one high value in a weighted transformed criterion alone is enough to make a location suitable.\nMinimum—Use when a single low value in a weighted transformed criterion should disqualify a location.\nWeighted overlay—Use to assign each criterion a percent influence that adds up to 100. The results are rescaled to the defined suitability scale. When the Weight by parameter value is set to Multiplier on the Settings tab, the assigned weights will be internally recalculated as percent influences.", "section": "Select a method"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#BGT

## 重疊方法 · 選擇一種方法

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-3

**english**　Overlay methods

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 分析和資料處理
- 空間分析器
- 建模者

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#BGT

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**text**　加權加權平均值 - 通過調整順序權重法參數值來探索決策設想方案。這樣,最終結果就更容易受到具有較高或較低價值的加權轉換標準的影響。理想點解決方案(TOPSIS) - 用每個單元格的合適性值計算出它與特定的理想和最不理想的解決方案之間的距離。對于每個單元格,該方法計算出與理想值和最不理想值之間的距離(數值的差異),然后將這些距離與輸出單元格值結合起來。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Ordered weighted averaging—Use to explore decision scenarios by adjusting the Order weights approach parameter value. This allows the final result to be more influenced by weighted transformed criteria that have higher or lower values.\nIdeal point solution (TOPSIS)—Use to calculate the suitability value of each cell by how close it is to a specified ideal and least desired solutions. For each cell, the method calculates the distance (difference in the values) from the ideal and from the least desirable values and then combines these distances into an output cell value.", "section": "Select a method"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#BGT

## 重疊方法 · 公式公式

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-6

**english**　Overlay methods

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 分析和資料處理
- 空間分析器
- 建模者

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#Q9L

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**text**　對于此方法, 從每個轉換標準中得出的單元格值乘以相應的重量。 將結果相加, 以創建輸出適合性光柵。 以公式表示, 每個位置的輸出值計算如下: \( OutRas = (Ras1\ times Weight1) + (Ras2\times Weight2) +\ldots + (RasN\times WeightN)\

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "For this method, the cell values from each transformed criteria are multiplied by their corresponding weight. The results are added together to create the output suitability raster.\nExpressed as a formula, the output value for each location is calculated as follows:\n\\(OutRas = (Ras1 \\times Weight1) + (Ras2 \\times Weight2) + \\ldots + (RasN \\times WeightN)\\)", "section": "Formula"}

**content_version**　3.8

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-7

**text**　下面的插圖顯示了將公式應用到兩個變換標準并適用不同重量值的結果。這些輸入將用于其他所有方法的例子中。 考慮左上角單元格。 InRas1 , 該位置的單元格值為 1. 1 乘以1.5 的重量, 數值為 1.5; InRas2 , 該位置的單元格值為 6.5, 因此乘以 2 的重量的結果是 13。 添加值1.5 和 13 的結果, 該位置的輸出單元格值為 14.5 。

For InRas2, the location has a cell value of 6.5, so the result of multiplying it by the weight of 2 is 13.\nAdding the values 1.5 and 13 results in an output cell value of 14.5 for that location.", "section": "Example"}

**text**　風力農場選址——在風力農場選址項目中,采用這種方法將若干標準匯總為單一的合適地圖;該方法可以說明如何擁有優異的風力資源能夠超過條件,如中等坡度(根據其指定的重要性)等條件; 酒園規劃-葡萄園場地規劃,加權總和法用于將自然氣候條件、成本相關因素和土壤特性納入最后適合性分數;高氣候和土壤適合性能可抵消較低費用條件,同時考慮到長期利潤。

The method can capture how having excellent wind resources can outweigh conditions, such as moderate slope, based on their assigned importance.\nWinery plantation planning—In vineyard site planning, the weighted sum method is used to combine natural climate conditions, cost-related factors, and soil properties into a final suitability score. High climate and soil suitability can offset less favorable cost conditions, considering long-term profits.", "section": "Potential applications"}

## 重疊方法 · 加權幾何平均值

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-9

**english**　Overlay methods

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 分析和資料處理
- 空間分析器
- 建模者

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#T5H

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**text**　使用重疊法參數的加權幾何平均值選項進行分析,使所有轉換的標準具有相對較高的數值,以達到高適度值。計算基于乘數,因此任何轉變標準中的低值將按比例減少最終適用性,而不能用其他轉變標準中較高值來補償。通常選擇它是為了保持低值的影響,將其作為一個限制因素,同時仍然包括來自其他投入的貢獻。加權指數性質使得能夠控制低值對結果的強烈影響。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Use the Weighted geometric mean option of the Overlay method parameter for analyses in which all transformed criteria have relatively high values to achieve a high suitability value.\nThe calculation is based on multiplication, so a low value in any transformed criterion will proportionally reduce the final suitability and cannot be compensated for by higher values in other transformed criteria. It is often chosen to maintain the influence of low values as a limiting factor, while still incorporating contributions from other inputs. The exponential nature of the weights allows control over how strongly low values affect the outcome.", "section": "Weighted geometric mean"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#T5H

## 重疊方法 · 公式公式

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-10

**english**　Overlay methods

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 分析和資料處理
- 空間分析器
- 建模者

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#ORN

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**text**　對于這個方法, 每一個轉換標準中的單元格值將提高到相應重量的功率。 結果會乘以倍數, 然后產品會升至所有重量之和的功率。 表達為公式, 首先計算所有重量的總和 :\ (Weights ⁇ sum = weight1 + Weight2 +\ldots + WeightN\) 下一步, 所有輸入單元格值都提高到與其相關重量的功率, 并且計算出這些數值的產物 :\(Vales ⁇ product = Ras1\\ weight1}\ times Ras2\\Weight2}\timets\ldots\times RasN ⁇ WeightN ⁇ ) 計算每個位置的最終輸出值的方法是將加權值的產值提高到所有重量的逆和。\ (OutRas = values ⁇ product ⁇ (1/ weights ⁇ sum)\\\\\

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "For this method, for each cell, the cell values from each transformed criterion are raised to the power of their corresponding weight. The results are multiplied and then the product is raised to the power of the sum of all the weights.\nExpressed as formulas, first the sum of all the weights is calculated:\n\\(Weights\\_sum = Weight1 + Weight2 + \\ldots + WeightN\\)\nNext, all of the input cell values are raised to the power of their associated weight, and the product of those values is calculated:\n\\(Values\\_product = Ras1^{Weight1} \\times Ras2^{Weight2} \\times \\ldots \\times RasN^{WeightN}\\)\nThe final output value for each location is calculated by raising the product of the weighted values to the inverse sum of all the weights.\n\\(OutRas = Values\\_product^{(1 / Weights\\_sum)}\\)", "section": "Formula"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#ORN

## 重疊方法 · 示例示例實例

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-11

**english**　Overlay methods

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 分析和資料處理
- 空間分析器
- 建模者

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#XGU

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**text**　下面的插圖顯示了對加權總和法中使用的相同輸入量和相同重量應用該公式的結果。 InRas1, 值1的左上角單元格提高至重量功率。11.5=1. 對InRas2, 重量使6.5的輸入單元格值提高至2的功率,結果為42.25。產值1和42.25是42.25。重量之和為1.5+2=3.5。該位置的最終輸出為42.25(1/3.5)  ⁇ 2.91。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The illustration below shows the result of applying this formula to the same inputs and the same weights used in the weighted sum method.\nFor InRas1, the upper left cell of value 1 is raised to the power of the weight. The result of 11.5 = 1.\nFor InRas2, the weight causes the input cell value of 6.5 to be raised to the power of 2, which results in a value of 42.25.\nThe product of 1 and 42.25 is 42.25. The sum of the weights is 1.5 + 2 = 3.5.\nThe final output for that location is 42.25(1 / 3.5) ≈ 2.91.", "section": "Example"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#XGU

## 重疊方法 · 潛在應用

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-12

**english**　Overlay methods

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 分析和資料處理
- 空間分析器
- 建模者

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#C1B

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**text**　生境適宜性指數——在生境建模中,多重標準——例如水質、溫度和光的可得性——采用加權幾何平均值加以合并,因此除非所有基本居住條件都有利,否則一個地點無法得分很高。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Applications for this method include the following:\nHabitat suitability index—In habitat modeling, multiple criteria—such as water quality, temperature, and light availability—are combined using a weighted geometric mean so a site cannot score highly unless all essential habitat conditions are favorable.\nAquaculture site suitability and yield estimation—In farming site evaluation, several environmental quality criteria are aggregated with a weighted geometric mean so overall suitability drops sharply when any critical environmental condition is poor.", "section": "Potential applications"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#C1B

## 重疊方法 · 最大

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-13

**english**　Overlay methods

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 分析和資料處理
- 空間分析器
- 建模者

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#AGK

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**text**　當加權轉換標準可以相互替代時,使用重疊法參數的最大選項,而您希望結果反映每個單元格最強的優勢或最高值,而不是總體平衡。當至少一個加權轉換標準具有高值時,即使其他加權轉換值較低,單元格獲得較高的輸出值。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Use the Maximum option of the Overlay method parameter when the weighted transformed criterion can substitute for one another and you want the result to reflect the strongest advantage or highest value at each cell rather than overall balance. A cell receives a high output value when at least one of the weighted transformed criterion has a high value, even if the other weighted transformed values are low.", "section": "Maximum"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#AGK

## 重疊方法 · 公式公式

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-14

**english**　Overlay methods

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 分析和資料處理
- 空間分析器
- 建模者

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#EHH

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**text**　對于每個輸入單元格,工具將指定該位置加權轉換標準中的最大值。計算每個位置加權轉換標準最大值的公式如下:\(OutRas = 最大 {(Ras1\ times Weight1),(Ras2\times Weight2),\ldots,(RasN\times WeightN) }

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "For each input cell, the tool will assign the largest value among the weighted transformed criterion at that location.\nThe formula for calculating the maximum of the weighted transformed criterion for each location is as follows:\n\\(OutRas = Maximum\\{(Ras1 \\times Weight1), (Ras2 \\times Weight2), \\ldots, (RasN \\times WeightN)\\}\\)", "section": "Formula"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#EHH

## 重疊方法 · 示例示例實例

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-15

**english**　Overlay methods

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 分析和資料處理
- 空間分析器
- 建模者

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#0PZ

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**text**　InRas1, 左上角單元格值乘1.5的重量,得出1.5的數值。InRas2, 單元格值乘以6.5的重量乘以2的重量,得出13. 這個方法返回兩個加權值中最大的值,因此輸出值為13。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The results of applying this formula to the same inputs and the same weights are as follows:\nFor InRas1, the upper left cell value of 1 is multiplied by the weight of 1.5, resulting in a value of 1.5. For InRas2, the cell value of 6.5 is multiplied by the weight of 2, which results in 13.\nThis method returns the largest of the two weighted values, so the output value will be 13.", "section": "Example"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#0PZ

## 重疊方法 · 潛在應用

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-16

**english**　Overlay methods

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 分析和資料處理
- 空間分析器
- 建模者

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**text**　這一方法的應用包括:礦物勘探-在鈷前景圖繪制中,使用最大覆蓋法將多種標準合并在一起,因此如果任何一個指標都有力地支持礦產潛力,就突出顯示一個電池。無線覆蓋繪圖——在無線電網絡規劃中,多個基站的信號強度激光儀使用最大覆蓋法結合,因此每個電池是該地點現有最強的覆蓋率。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Applications for this method include the following:\nMineral exploration—In cobalt prospectivity mapping, multiple criteria are combined using the Maximum overlay method so a cell is highlighted if any one indicator strongly supports mineral potential.\nWireless coverage mapping—In radio network planning, signal strength rasters from multiple base stations are combined using the Maximum overlay method so each cell represents the strongest available coverage at that location.", "section": "Potential applications"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#XFJ

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-20

**english**　Overlay methods

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#NJL

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**text**　生境適宜性——生境建模、食物和水的供應、土地使用類型和人類擾動都被認為是關鍵的限制因素,它們結合使用最低限度重疊法,因此如果任何基本生境條件差的話,細胞的等級較低;太陽能農場與多個子模型坐在一起——為太陽資源適合性、可及性和環境影響繪制了三個子模型適合性地圖;由于太陽能資源適宜性被視為一個限制性因素,所以采用最低限度方法得出最后的合適性,確保即使其他條件有利,太陽能潛力較差的地點仍然不合適。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Applications for this method include the following:\nHabitat suitability—In habitat modeling, food and water availability, land-use type, and human disturbance are all considered key limiting factors. They are combined using the Minimum overlay method so a cell is rated low if any essential habitat conditions are poor.\nSolar farm siting with multiple submodels—Three submodels suitability maps are created for solar resource suitability, accessibility, and environmental impact. Because solar resource suitability is treated as a limiting factor, the Minimum method is used to derive the final suitability, ensuring that locations with poor solar potential remain low in suitability even if other conditions are favorable.", "section": "Potential applications"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#NJL

## 重疊方法 · 加權重疊

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-21

**english**　Overlay methods

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 分析和資料處理
- 空間分析器
- 建模者

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#GXO

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**text**　使用重疊方法參數的加權覆蓋選項, 當您想要每個標準作為最終輸出的百分比對結果作出貢獻時, 請使用重疊法參數的加權重疊選項。 在使用此方法時, 通常選擇百分比作為設置標簽上按參數計算的重量。 因此, 加權為百分數, 且必須計算到 100 。 每個百分數表示每個標準相對于其他標準對最后產出的影響強度。 然后將合并結果重新縮放到設置標簽上的Set 適合度比例參數定義的合適性尺度, 如 0 和 1 或 1 和 10. 。 這確保合適的地圖值保持在一個一致范圍內, 使您能夠直接比較不同輸入標準或加權設置的結果。 當設定參數值乘以乘積時 。設置標簽上顯示的加權數將按百分比影響內部重新計算。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Use the Weighted overlay option of the Overlay method parameter when you want each criterion to contribute to the result as a percentage of the final output. Often, when using this method, Percent is selected as the Weight by parameter on the Settings tab. As a result, the weights are percentages and must sum to 100. Each percent value indicates how strongly each criterion influences the final output relative to the other criteria.\nThe combined result is then rescaled to the suitability scale defined by the Set suitability scale parameter on the Settings tab, such as 0 and 1 or 1 and 10. This ensures that the suitability map values stay within a consistent range, allowing you to directly compare results generated from different input criteria or weighting settings.\nWhen the Weight by parameter value is set to Multiplier on the Settings tab, the assigned weights will be internally recalculated as percent influences.", "section": "Weighted overlay"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#GXO

## 重疊方法 · 公式公式

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-22

**english**　Overlay methods

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 分析和資料處理

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**text**　對于每個位置,在每個轉換標準中相應的單元格值乘以為百分比選項提供的值。所有加權值都加在一起。然后將結果縮放到確定的輸出范圍。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "For each location, corresponding cell values in each transformed criterion are multiplied by the value provided for the Percent option. All the weighted values are added together. That result is then scaled to the defined output range.", "section": "Formula"}

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#W9P

## 重疊方法 · 公式公式

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-23

**english**　Overlay methods

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 分析和資料處理
- 空間分析器
- 建模者

**kind**　有序步驟

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#W9P

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**steps**　
1. 每個位置的輸出值計算如下 :\(混合Value = (Ras1\ times Percent1) +(Ras2\times Percent2) +\ldots + (RasN\ times Pern%N)\)
2. 然后使用以下公式對加權總和輸出進行重新排序 :\( OutRas ={ fromssolution} + ({CombinedValue} -{combinedValue ⁇ min})\ cdot\ mlarge\ frac{Tosult- FromSergile_CombinedValue ⁇ {max} - 組合Value ⁇ min}

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"steps": ["The output value for each location is calculated as follows: \\(CombinedValue = (Ras1 \\times Percent1) + (Ras2 \\times Percent2) + \\ldots + (RasN \\times PercentN)\\)", "Then the weighted summed output is rescaled using the formula below: \\(OutRas = {FromScale} + ({CombinedValue} - {CombinedValue}_{\\min}) \\cdot \\large \\frac {ToScale - FromScale}{CombinedValue_{\\max} - CombinedValue_{\\min}}\\)"], "section": "Formula"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#W9P

## 重疊方法 · 示例示例實例

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-24

**english**　Overlay methods

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 分析和資料處理
- 空間分析器
- 建模者

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#LKM

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**text**　應用此公式的結果如下。 在此示例中選擇的輸出尺度為 1- 10 。 對于 InRas1, 值1 的左上角單元格乘以小數點百分比 0.428 = 0.428;對于InRas2, 單元格值為 6.5 乘以小數百分比 0. 572 = 3.718。 這兩個數值的總和是 4.146。 然后, 工具對所有九個輸入位置進行這些計算。 結果最小值為 3. 197, 最大值為 7.112。 在以下該位置的最后輸出值中將這些值應用到公式結果:\ (\ begin{ 調整} OutRas & 1 + (4. 146 - 3. 1972)\ cdot\ frac{10 - 1 + *11: 24 - 3.1972}\\\  ⁇  3.2\end{調整}

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "The result of applying this formula follows. The output scale chosen for this example is 1-10.\nFor InRas1, the upper left cell of value 1 multiplied by the decimal percentage 0.428 = 0.428. For InRas2, the cell value of 6.5 multiplied by the decimal percentage 0.572 = 3.718.\nThe sum of these two values is 4.146.\nThe tool then performs these calculations for all nine input locations. The resulting minimum value is 3.197, and the maximum value is 7.112.\nApplying these values to the formula results in the following final output value for this location:\n\\(\\begin{aligned}OutRas &= 1 + (4.146 - 3.1972) \\cdot \\frac{10 - 1}{7.1124 - 3.1972} \\\\ &= 3.2\\end{aligned}\\)", "section": "Example"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#LKM

## 重疊方法 · 定序加權平均平均數

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-26

**english**　Overlay methods

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 分析和資料處理
- 空間分析器
- 建模者

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#VXO

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**text**　在想要強調高加權轉換標準時,或者在想探索不同權衡和補償水平的敏感結果如何對不同的權衡和補償水平產生敏感結果時,使用重疊法參數的有順序加權加權平均率選項;在想強調高加權轉換標準標準標準值時,或在想探探在不同權衡和補償水平上是如何得出敏感結果的時,如果將每個單元格中從最高到最低的加權加權加權平均平均率(OWA)首先將每個單元格的加權換算標準值從最高到最低。然后對排名值應用一套單獨的順序加權權數,以控制對最佳、中等或最最差轉換標準的影響程度。如果給每個單元格指定了1至最高加權轉換標準和0的訂單權重,則使用該能力定制定序權權設置來控制在加權轉換標準之間如何抵消低數值,并估計不同重疊方法的結果。其它的,奧華婦女協會的行為類似于最大法,加權高轉換標準值的影響最大。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Use the Ordered weighted averaging option of the Overlay method parameter when you want to emphasize the high weighted transformed criterion or when you want to explore how sensitive results are to different levels of trade-off and compensation\nUnlike methods that apply fixed weights to fixed criteria positions, Ordered weighted averaging (OWA) first sorts the weighted transformed criterion values at each cell from highest to lowest. It then applies a separate set of order weights to the ranked values that control how much influence the best, middle, or worst transformed criterion has.\nUse the ability to customize the order weight settings to control how high values can offset low values across the weighted transformed criteria and to approximate the results of different overlay methods.\nIf you assign an order weight of 1 to the highest weighted transformed criterion and 0 to all others, OWA behaves like the Maximum method where the weighted high transformed criterion values have the most influence.", "section": "Ordered weighted averaging"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#VXO

## 重疊方法 · 定序加權平均平均數

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-27

**english**　Overlay methods

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 分析和資料處理
- 空間分析器
- 建模者

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#VXO

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**text**　如果將順序重量為1至最低加權轉換標準值和0至所有其它標準值,則OWA的行為方式與最小法相同,即加權低轉換標準值明顯限制輸出。如果對所有排名值給予相等的排序權重,OWA的行為方式類似于加權總和方法,其中加權轉換標準對結果的貢獻是均勻的。有三種方法指定順序權重:自定義、量化制導和最大倍數。當將順序權重接近參數值設定到自定義選項時,您可以手工輸入加權。量化制導和最大誘變選項自動產生訂單權重。兩種方法都使用一個Orness參數值來控制形狀。Orness 描述重疊值有多樂觀。 高值會給最高級加權轉換標準值帶來更大的影響力。 低值會給最底層加權轉換標準值帶來更大的影響力。 兩個自動方法之間的差別是順序加權的分布方式。 量化制導法產生一種定期增加或減少等級模式, 而最大倍增制成一個更分散的排名模式。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "If you assign an order weight of 1 to the lowest weighted transformed criterion and 0 to all others, OWA behaves like the Minimum method where the weighted low transformed criterion values strongly limit the output.\nIf you give equal order weights to all ranked values, OWA behaves like a Weighted sum method, in which weighted transformed criteria contribute evenly to the result.\nThere are three ways to specify the order weights: Custom, Quantifier-guided, and Maximum entropy.\nWhen the Order weights approach parameter value is set to the Custom option, you can enter the weights manually.\nThe Quantifier-guided and Maximum entropy options derive order weights automatically. Both approaches use an Orness parameter value to control the shape of the order weights. Orness describes how optimistic the overlay is. Higher values give more influence to the highest-ranked weighted transformed criterion value. Lower values give more influence to the lowest-ranked weighted transformed criterion value. The difference between the two automatic approaches is how the order weights are distributed. Quantifier-guided approach produces a regular increasing or decreasing rank pattern while Maximum entropy produces a more dispersed rank pattern.", "section": "Ordered weighted averaging"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#VXO

## 重疊方法 · 公式公式

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-28

**english**　Overlay methods

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 分析和資料處理
- 空間分析器
- 建模者

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#L7Q

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**text**　對于每個輸入光柵,每個單元格值乘以該轉換標準所確定的重量值。接下來,對于每個單元格的位置,將加權轉換的數值從最高排到最低。將分類的值乘以順序加權數,然后將它們相加,得出該單元格的最終輸出值。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "For each input raster, multiply every cell value by the value of the defined weight for that transformed criterion. Next, for each cell location, rank the weighted transformed values from highest to lowest. Multiply the sorted values by the order weights and then sum them together to produce the final output value for that cell.", "section": "Formula"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#L7Q

## 重疊方法 · 公式公式

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-29

**english**　Overlay methods

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 分析和資料處理
- 空間分析器
- 建模者

**kind**　有序步驟

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#L7Q

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**steps**　
1. 首先,計算每個輸入位置的加權輸入值 。\ (加權投入1 = Ras1 = Ras1 \ times weight1\\)\ (加權投入2 = Ras2 = times weight2\)\ (= ldots\)\ (加權投入N = RasN = times WeightN\)
2. 接下來,加權值將從最高排到最低排。\ (( 弧度 ⁇ 值 =  ⁇ Rank1( 加權指數 1) , 列2 ( 加權指數 2) ,\ doldts, RankN( 加權指數 ) \\ )
3. 順序加權值列表將作為輸入提供, 也可以由工具生成 。\( 列表\\ 重量\ 值= @ RankWeight1, RankWeight2,\ ldots, RankWeightN ⁇ )
4. 順序加權值乘以排位加權值,然后相加以提供該單元格位置在最終輸出光柵中的數值。\\ [\ begin{rary}} outRas = (RankWeight1\ times Rank1(加權輸入1))+ (RankWeight2\ times Rance2(加權輸入2))\\\\phonom{OutRas = @+\ldots + (RankWeightN\times RankN(加權輸入N))\end{ary}\]

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"steps": ["First, calculate the weighted input value for each input location. \\(WeightedInput1 = Ras1 \\times Weight1\\) \\(WeightedInput2 = Ras2 \\times Weight2\\) \\(\\ldots\\) \\(WeightedInputN = RasN \\times WeightN\\)", "Next, the weighted values will be ranked from highest to lowest. \\(Ranked\\_values = \\{Rank1(WeightedInput1), Rank2(WeightedInput2), \\ldots, RankN(WeightedInputN)\\}\\)", "A list of order weight values will either be provided as input or can be generated by the tool. \\(List\\ of\\ weight\\ values = \\{RankWeight1, RankWeight2, \\ldots, RankWeightN\\}\\)", "The order weight values are multiplied by the ranked weighted values and then summed together to provide the value for that cell location in the final output raster. \\[ \\begin{array}{l} OutRas = (RankWeight1 \\times Rank1(WeightedInput1)) + (RankWeight2 \\times Rank2(WeightedInput2)) \\\\ \\phantom{OutRas = {}} + \\ldots + (RankWeightN \\times RankN(WeightedInputN)) \\end{array} \\]"], "section": "Formula"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#L7Q

## 重疊方法 · 示例示例實例

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-30

**english**　Overlay methods

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 分析和資料處理
- 空間分析器
- 建模者

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#LVU

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**text**　對示例輸入應用這一方法得出以下產出: InRas1, 左上角單元格位置的值乘以重量,結果為1x1.5=1.5;InRas2, 結果為6.5x2=13。從最高到最低的這個位置的加權值為13和1.5。給定的順序權重0.8和0.2適用于等級值。InRas2, 結果為13x0.8=10.4。InRas1,結果為:1.5x0.2=0.3。這兩個數值的總和是10.4+0.3=10.7。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Applying this method to the example inputs results in the following output:\nFor InRas1, the value of the upper left cell location is multiplied by the weight, and the result of 1 x 1.5 = 1.5. For InRas2, the result is 6.5 x 2 = 13.\nThe weighted values for this location ranked from highest to lowest are 13 and 1.5.\nThe provided order weights 0.8 and 0.2 are applied to the ranked values. For InRas2, the result is 13 x 0.8 = 10.4. For InRas1, the result is: 1.5 x 0.2 = 0.3.\nThe sum of these two values is 10.4 + 0.3 = 10.7.", "section": "Example"}

**content_version**　3.8

**source_language**　en

**edition_source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/3.8/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#LVU

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-31

**english**　Overlay methods

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 分析和資料處理
- 空間分析器
- 建模者

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#V12

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**text**　綜合農業規劃——通過應用不同組合的順序權重,您可以比較產出適合性圖,該圖可作以下工作: 對可靠的長期生產實施嚴格的限制標準; 突出近期擴展的巨大潛力機會; 展示強調中宜地區、避免極端的平衡戰略,這些戰略可有助于風險反向規劃; 滑坡易感性測繪——你可以調整訂單權重,以控制在斜坡、降雨量、地質和土地覆蓋等滑坡因素之間允許多少權衡。這樣就可以比較下列易變地圖: 適用嚴格、限制性的因素規則,很少或根本沒有權衡。 使用更寬松的規則。檢驗在標準中允許部分補償的中間戰略。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Applications for this method include the following:\nIntegrated agricultural planning—By applying different combinations of order weights, you can compare an output suitability map that does the following: Enforces strict limiting criteria for reliable long-term production. Highlights high-potential opportunities for near-term expansion. Reveals balanced strategies that emphasize moderately suitable areas, avoiding extremes, which can be useful for risk-averse planning.\nLandslide susceptibility mapping—You can adjust the order weights to control how much trade-off is allowed among landslide factors, such as slope, rainfall, geology, and land cover. This makes it possible to compare susceptibility maps that do the following: Apply a strict, limiting factor rule with little or no trade-off. Use a more permissive rule where strong factors can have greater influence. Test intermediate strategies that allow partial compensation among criteria.", "section": "Potential applications"}

## 重疊方法 · 理想點解決辦法

**id**　8e38c0ef9102080acd-32

**english**　Overlay methods

**chapter**　
- 幫助幫助幫助
- 分析和資料處理
- 空間分析器
- 建模者

**kind**　說明

**source**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html#PTP

**source_en**　https://doc.esri.com/en/arcgis-pro/latest/help/analysis/spatial-analyst/suitability-modeler/overlay-methods.html

**text**　在您想要平衡的折衷解決方案時,使用重疊方法參數的“理想點”選項。該選項確定同時傾向于接近理想條件且遠離不可取條件的地點。與其他方法不同,這種方法可以優化一個以上因素。對每項標準而言,一個理想正點(最偏好)和一個理想負點(最偏偏偏)來自適用于標準的轉換。“理想正點”和“理想負點”(最偏偏偏)。“理想負點”往往是改變的標準的最大和最小。但是,有些情況,例如適用高斯函數可以用來轉換方面,其中中點可能是理想的積極點,低高標準值被指定為理想點。負點。您通過選擇的轉換控制理想正值和負值。

**translation**　英文原頁機譯，請核對原文

**original**　{"text": "Use the Ideal point solutions (TOPSIS) option of the Overlay method parameter when you want a balanced compromise solution. This option identifies locations that simultaneously favor being near desirable conditions and are far from undesirable conditions. Unlike the others, this method can optimize more than one factor.\nAn Ideal Positive point (most preferred) and an Ideal Negative point (least preferred) for each criterion are derived from the transformation applied to the criteria. The Ideal Positive and Ideal Negative points often are the maximum and minimum of the transformed criteria. However, there are certain situations such as applying the Gaussian function that might be used to transform aspect, in which the midpoint may be the ideal positive point with the low and high criterion values being assigned as the ideal negative points. You control the ideal positive and negative values through the transformation you select.", "section": "Ideal point solutions (TOPSIS)"}
